<div id="crazy-8s-reverse-stun">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">REVERSE STUN [x3]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Game</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Specific & Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/00-core-game-reverse-stun.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Play in front of the Player You are Targeting.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>Target any Player. When Their next PLAYABLE TURN arrives, this Card deactivates, and the Direction of Play will instantly Reverse, returning to the previous Player.
<div class="tip-box">
<strong>TIP:</strong>
<ul>
<li>When Stacking Chaos Cards Like Reverse Stun, Stun and Steal Turn, literally Stack them on top of each other in front of the Targeted Player.</li>
<li>Then when Their Turn arrives, Deactivate the Top Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>After the Targeted Player’s Turn has been Skipped due to this Card’s Effect, place this Card on top of the Discards.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol start="2">
<li>The Targeted Player chooses a Colour to continue.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Not only does Reverse Stun Stack with itself, but it also Stacks with Stun, Steal Turn and other Card Effects that prevent PLAYABLE TURNs. When this happens, the last Card played Must be finalised before the previous one Can be [See: <a href="/00-a-universal-game-notes#important-notes-03" class="matrix-link">Universal Game Notes: Important Notes: Rule 3</a>].
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If the Targeted Player has a Skip or Draw Effect Card used on them before Stun Can deactivate, they will Skip an Additional Turn per Card Effect.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>Once Resolved, and a Colour is Chosen, the Next Player May Play any Coloured Reverse Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Skips Their Turn instead.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be played as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
<li>Targeted Players Can still RESPOND IN KIND [passing Stacked Effects onto the next Player] to Draw Effects by continuing the Stack, but Cannot use Counter Class Chaos Cards.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-roulette">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">

<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Extension</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Normal Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>

</div>

<li>All Players Place 1 Coloured Card [Any] and 1 Chaos Card [Any] in a Pile.</li>
<li>The Pile is Shuffled and Dealt back to all Players one Card at a time.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">

</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">

<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
<li>Cannot be Your last Card.</li>
</ul>

<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Unknown Targeting</span></strong></li>

</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>When this Card is revealed, any Royal Knight Class Chaos Cards in Player’s Hands Must be revealed to the group.</li>
<li>Optionally, if all four Royal Knight Class Chaos Cards are NOT revealed, the User Can choose to search the Deck or Discards for any Royal Knight Class Chaos Card that May be there, choose one and give it to any Player with the least number of Royal Knight Class Chaos Cards in Their Hand [including themselves if possible].</li>
</ol>
</div>

</ol>
</div>

</ul>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-soloversal-engine-overdrive">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">THE ARCHITECT OF THE SOLOVERSE: SOLOVERSAL ENGINE OVERDRIVE [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Architect of the Soloverse Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive & Architect Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/19-soloversal-engine-overdrive.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a PLAYABLE TURN.</li>
<li>This Card Can be Used Two Times in Any Game.</li>
<li>The First Time You Use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Second Time You Use this Card, resolve it's Effects then Remove this Card from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>If any Player is Forced to Draw a Total of 20 Cards or more from a Draw Effect, reveal this Card to Change the Draw Effect to a Group Targeting Effect instead.</li>
<li>In Play Order, Starting with the Targeted Player, all other Players [except this Card's User] Must now draw the same number of Cards as the Targeted Player.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can only be Countered <strong>*** At the Moment of Activation ***</strong> by the Architect Chaos Card: <a href="#crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox" class="return-link">Unstable Soloversal Paradox</a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with a Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Architect of the Soloverse Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive & Architect Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/19-soloversal-singularity.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a PLAYABLE TURN.</li>
<li>This Card Can be Used Two Times in Any Game.</li>
<li>The First Time You Use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Second Time You Use this Card, resolve it's Effects then Remove this Card from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>For the next Round of Turns, all Players give You Their Cards.</li>
<li>You get to choose what Cards each Player Plays for Their Turn.</li>
<li>This includes not Playing Cards at all, forcing them to Draw instead and wasting any potentially powerful Chaos Cards early.</li>
</ol>
</div>

</ul>
</div>

<ul>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with a Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-soloversal-destruction">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">THE ARCHITECT OF THE SOLOVERSE: SOLOVERSAL DESTRUCTION [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Doomsday Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Trap Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Instant Activation</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/15-doomsday-expansion-trap-soloversal Destruction.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ul>
<li>When revealed, the SOLOVERSE will be destroyed resulting in all Players being Removed from the Game, ending the game.</li>
<li>But there is hope; You get one chance...
<ol>
<li>Temporarily remove Reality Bomb from the Deck.</li>
<li>Reveal the top Card:
<ol type="a">
<li>If that Card is any Chaos Card [including a Face-Up Trap Class Chaos Card], the Reality Bomb has been averted, for now... Remove Said Chaos Card from the Game [the cost of survival].</li>
<li>If that Card is Any Other Type of Card... The Game Ends, and Everyone Loses!!! [See <a href="/01-a-crazy-8s-game-rulebook#victory-conditions-05" class="matrix-link">(01-A) Soloverse – Crazy 8’s (Game Rulebook): Victory Conditions: Rule 5].</a></li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Can only be Countered by Its Own Effect.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ul>
<li>Only if You Countered it, continue to Draw if required.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>If You Countered it, Shuffle Reality Bomb back into the Deck.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-soloverse-1-of-3-sol">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">SOLOVERSE 1 OF 3: SOL [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Singularity Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Unification Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Self & Specific Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/07-singularity-expansion-soloverse-1-of-3-sol.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>To use one of these Card’s Effects, Place it on the Table in front of You [as proof of ownership], resolve its Effect and Then Return it to Your Hand [if required].</li>
<li>Activate before You start a PLAYABLE TURN.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>Choose 1 of the following Effects:
<ol type="a">
<li>Swap 1 Card in Your Hand for 1 random Card from any other Player [in order to collect all the Unification Class Chaos Cards to win].</li>
<li>Swap this Face-Up Unification Card for 1 random Card from any other Player [in order to win via normal means].</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Then continue Your Turn as normal.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>OPTIONAL ADVANCED UNIQUE EFFECT NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>As a group, You May choose whether to use the Unique Effects listed on each individual Unification Class Chaos Card.</li>
<li>Using these Unique Effects Can become complicated and is intended to add increased Difficulty for those up for the Challenge!</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>OPTIONAL ADVANCED UNIQUE EFFECT: REPARATION:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>If You Lose this Card to another Player by any means, You Must Draw 1 Card.</li>
<li>Furthermore, if You gain this Card from another Player by any means [not including Drawing it from the Deck], You Can Discard 1 Card.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>All Unification Class Card Effects Cannot be Countered, Including Their Special Victory Condition.</li>
<li>They Cannot be played as a Pure Chaos Card. They Cannot even be Discarded, by any means [e.g.: Black Hole].</li>
<li>They Cannot leave Player’s Hands, except by Card Swapping Effects [e.g.: A Unification Class Chaos Card Effect, Paradigm Shift, Double Under, etc…].</li>
<li>You Can only use 1 Unification Effect per Turn, and it Can only be used Once each Turn; however, You Can use it on additional Turns afterwards.</li>
<li>You Cannot use another Chaos Card on the Same Turn You activate a Unification Class Chaos Card; and vice versa.</li>
<li>All Unification Class Chaos Cards Cannot affect the same Players two Activations in a row; regardless of who is the User and who is the Target.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>All Unification Class Chaos Cards Can only Target the other Player every second Turn.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="special-victory-condition-note-box">
<strong>SPECIAL VICTORY CONDITION:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Possessing all three Unification Class Chaos Cards at once Instantly Wins You the game [regardless of the number of Cards You possess; or whose Turn it actually is].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>PENALTY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>There are 3 Unification Class Chaos Cards in the Deck at the Start of the game. If the Deck does NOT contain the correct amount of Unification Class Chaos Cards when compared to the amount revealed, someone has played Theirs incorrectly – either by Discarding or NOT Revealing it.</li>
<li>If someone is found to have either Illegally Discarded [e.g.: Black Hole, Discard Chaos Cards] or NOT Revealed Their Unification Class Chaos Card [during the Effect of the Call of the Soloverse Trap Class Chaos Card], they Must Forfeit Their next 5 PLAYABLE TURNs.</li>
<li>Then Retrieve the Unification Class Chaos Card from the Discards and give it to a Player with the Least amount of Unification Class Chaos Cards in Their Hand.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-soloverse-2-of-3-ove">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">SOLOVERSE 2 OF 3: OVE [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Singularity Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Unification Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Self & Specific Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/07-singularity-expansion-soloverse-2-of-3-ove.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>To use one of these Card’s Effects, Place it on the Table in front of You [as proof of ownership], resolve its Effect and Then Return it to Your Hand [if required].</li>
<li>Activate before You start a PLAYABLE TURN.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>Choose 1 of the following Effects:
<ol type="a">
<li>Swap 1 Card in Your Hand for 1 random Card from any other Player [in order to collect all the Unification Class Chaos Cards to win].</li>
<li>Swap this Face-Up Unification Card for 1 random Card from any other Player [in order to win via normal means].</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Then continue Your Turn as normal.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>OPTIONAL ADVANCED UNIQUE EFFECT NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>As a group, You May choose whether to use the Unique Effects listed on each individual Unification Class Chaos Card.</li>
<li>Using these Unique Effects Can become complicated and is intended to add increased Difficulty for those up for the Challenge!</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>OPTIONAL ADVANCED UNIQUE EFFECT: RETRIBUTION:</strong>
<ol>
<li>If You gain this Card from another Player by any means [not including Drawing it from the Deck], You Must Draw 3 Cards.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>All Unification Class Card Effects Cannot be Countered, Including Their Special Victory Condition.</li>
<li>They Cannot be played as a Pure Chaos Card. They Cannot even be Discarded, by any means [e.g.: Black Hole].</li>
<li>They Cannot leave Player’s Hands, except by Card Swapping Effects [e.g.: A Unification Class Chaos Card Effect, Paradigm Shift, Double Under, etc…].</li>
<li>You Can only use 1 Unification Effect per Turn, and it Can only be used Once each Turn; however, You Can use it on additional Turns afterwards.</li>
<li>You Cannot use another Chaos Card on the Same Turn You activate a Unification Class Chaos Card; and vice versa.</li>
<li>All Unification Class Chaos Cards Cannot affect the same Players two Activations in a row; regardless of who is the User and who is the Target.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>All Unification Class Chaos Cards Can only Target the other Player every second Turn.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="special-victory-condition-note-box">
<strong>SPECIAL VICTORY CONDITION:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Possessing all three Unification Class Chaos Cards at once Instantly Wins You the game [regardless of the number of Cards You possess; or whose Turn it actually is].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>PENALTY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>There are 3 Unification Class Chaos Cards in the Deck at the Start of the game. If the Deck does NOT contain the correct amount of Unification Class Chaos Cards when compared to the amount revealed, someone has played Theirs incorrectly – either by Discarding or NOT Revealing it.</li>
<li>If someone is found to have either Illegally Discarded [e.g.: Black Hole, Discard Chaos Cards] or NOT Revealed Their Unification Class Chaos Card [during the Effect of the Call of the Soloverse Trap Class Chaos Card], they Must Forfeit Their next 5 PLAYABLE TURNs.</li>
<li>Then Retrieve the Unification Class Chaos Card from the Discards and give it to a Player with the Least amount of Unification Class Chaos Cards in Their Hand.</li>
</ul>
</div>

</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-steal-turn">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">STEAL TURN [x3]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Game</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Specific Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/00-core-game-steal-turn.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>Target any Player. When Their next PLAYABLE TURN arrives, this Card deactivates, and You Steal Their Turn.
<div class="tip-box">
<strong>TIP:</strong>
<ul>
<li>When Stacking Chaos Cards Like Reverse Stun, Stun and Steal Turn, literally Stack them on top of each other in front of the Targeted Player.</li>
<li>Then when Their Turn arrives, Deactivate the Top Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>After the Targeted Player’s Turn has been Stolen due to this Card’s Effect, place this Card on top of the Discards.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol start="2">
<li>The Targeted Player chooses a Colour to continue.</li>
<li>Take Your Turn as normal.</li>
<li>Play Continues normally from You.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be Countered.</li>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
<li>Targeted Players Can still RESPOND IN KIND [passing Stacked Effects onto the next Player] to Draw Effects by continuing the Stack, but Cannot use Counter Class Chaos Cards.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Not only does Steal Turn Stack with itself, but it also Stacks with Reverse Stun, Stun and other Card Effects that prevent PLAYABLE TURNs. When this happens, the last Card played Must be finalised before the previous one Can be [See: <a href="/00-a-universal-game-notes#important-notes-03" class="matrix-link">Universal Game Notes: Important Notes: Rule 3</a>].
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If the Targeted Player has a Skip or Draw Effect Card used on them before Stun Can deactivate, they will Skip an Additional Turn per Card Effect.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">

</div>

<div class="tip-box">
<strong>TIP:</strong>
<ul>

</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ol>
</div>

</ul>
</div>

<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>

When this happens, the last Card played Must be finalised before the previous one Can be [See: <a href="/00-a-universal-game-notes#important-notes-03" class="matrix-link">Universal Game Notes: Important Notes: Rule 3</a>].
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>

<div id="crazy-8s-substitution-stone">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">SUBSTITUTION STONE [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Catacysm Extension</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive & Ancient Stone Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Unknown Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/16-cataclysm-expansion-2-of-6-substitution-stone.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>To use an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Turn it Sideways.</li>
<li>Each Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card’s Effect Can only be used Once Each Turn; however, You Can use it on additional Turns afterwards.</li>
<li>Use of Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects do NOT count towards Your one Chaos Card per Turn Restriction.</li>
<li>Activate before You start a PLAYABLE TURN.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>On any PLAYABLE TURN, You May Swap any Player’s Hand Cards or Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards with any other Player [including Yourself], then proceed with Your Turn as normal.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot be Countered, Including Their Special Victory Condition.</li>
<li>Only 1 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Can be used each Turn [compare BULK POSSESSION NOTES].</li>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot affect the same Players two Activations in a row, regardless of who is the User and who is the Target.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If someone uses the Teleportation Stone to take control of Your seat and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, the Teleportation Stone Cannot reclaim them. You Must use the Teleportation Stone to Target another Player.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Individual Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards Can only Target the other Player every second Turn.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards May NOT count towards Your Hand’s Card count [so You Can still win with no Cards even if You possess one], but they Can still be stolen via card Swapping means; however, if doing this, You Can only Swap Hand Cards or Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If chosen, Paradigm Shift’s Group Targeting Swap Effect Can target all Player’s Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards instead of Their Hand Cards [the Swap includes All Players Except Previous Unrestriction Stone Users, regardless of whether they currently own an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card or not].</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>Furthermore, Hand Discarding Effects affect both Hand and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. However, Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards are collected, Shuffled and Re-Dealt according to Their Setup Rules.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Card Effects like Black Hole.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>BULK POSSESSION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>2 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 3 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES NOT include the ability to play Another Chaos Card or Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>3 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card but DOES NOT include the ability to play another Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>4 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 5 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card AND another Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card.</li>
<li><strong>5 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>User:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>At any point during another Player’s Turn, Attempt to take possession of the last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card. This Must be Attempted Once, and only Once, between Your PLAYABLE TURNs.</li>
<li>Failure to Attempt to do so or being prevented from doing so will result in all Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards being Collected and Re-Dealt according to Their Setup Rules.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li><strong>The Last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Holder:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>IF the User attempts to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Slap the Table to Defend Yourself.</li>
<li>IF You Slap the Table and the User did NOT attempt to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, You Forfeit it to them.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="special-victory-condition-note-box">
<strong>SPECICAL VICTORY CONDITION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>6 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> Possessing All 6 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards at once instantly Wins You the game [regardless of the number of Cards You possess; or whose Turn it actually is].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>NOTES: Any time a Player is Removed from or Restored to the Game by any means, Collect all Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, Shuffle and Re-Deal them to all Players still currently In the Game. But You Must maintain Play Order [as in the Player that caused this Removal/Restoration Effect (either directly or indirectly) Must perform the above Shuffle & Re-Deal of the Ancient Stones; and as the Dealer, they are last to receive one].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Special Victory Condition now Activates with by possessing all 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards in Play [Compare <a href="/01-a-crazy-8s-game-rulebook" class="matrix-link">(01-A) Soloverse 01-A Crazy 8's Game Rulebook: Cataclysm Expansion Additional Setup Rules</a>].</li>
</ul>
</div>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-system-absolute-zero">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">

<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Architect of the Soloverse Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Architect Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Persistent</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/19-system-absolute-zero.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a PLAYABLE TURN.</li>
<li>To use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Effect lasts for the rest of the game, or until it is Deactivated or Overwritten.
<ul>
<li>You May Deactivate this Card before you start any PLAYABLE TURN. To Deactivate this Card, Simply Remove it from the Game.</li>
<li>Should a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect be Activated, it will Overwrite this one, Removing it from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Only Chaos Cards Can be Played for the Rest of the Game.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can only be Countered <strong>*** At the Moment of Activation ***</strong> by the Architect Chaos Card: <a href="#crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox" class="return-link">Unstable Soloversal Paradox</a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with a Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-teleportation-stone">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">TELEPORTATION STONE [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Cataclysm Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive & Ancient Stone Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Self & Specific Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/16-cataclysm-expansion-4-of-6-teleportation-stone.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>To use an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Turn it Sideways.</li>
<li>Each Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card’s Effect Can only be used Once Each Turn; however, You Can use it on additional Turns afterwards.</li>
<li>Use of Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects do NOT count towards Your one Chaos Card per Turn Restriction.</li>
<li>Activate before You start a PLAYABLE TURN.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>On any PLAYABLE TURN, You May choose to Swap Seats with any other Player [You Must each leave Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards behind but take Your Hand Cards with You] and then proceed with Your Turn as normal.</li>
<li>Play continues from Your new location.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="note-box">
<ul>
<li>You Cannot Target Previous Unrestriction Stone Users.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot be Countered, Including Their Special Victory Condition.</li>
<li>Only 1 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Can be used each Turn [compare BULK POSSESSION NOTES].</li>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot affect the same Players two Activations in a row, regardless of who is the User and who is the Target.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If someone uses the Teleportation Stone to take control of Your seat and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, the Teleportation Stone Cannot reclaim them. You Must use the Teleportation Stone to Target another Player.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Simply Swap Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards.</li>
<li>Individual Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards Can only Target the other Player every second Turn.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards May NOT count towards Your Hand’s Card count [so You Can still win with no Cards even if You possess one], but they Can still be stolen via card Swapping means; however, if doing this, You Can only Swap Hand Cards or Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If chosen, Paradigm Shift’s Group Targeting Swap Effect Can target all Player’s Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards instead of Their Hand Cards [the Swap includes All Players Except Previous Unrestriction Stone Users, regardless of whether they currently own an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card or not].</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>Furthermore, Hand Discarding Effects affect both Hand and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. However, Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards are collected, Shuffled and Re-Dealt according to Their Setup Rules.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Card Effects like Black Hole.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>BULK POSSESSION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>2 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 3 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES NOT include the ability to play Another Chaos Card or Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>3 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card but DOES NOT include the ability to play another Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>4 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 5 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card AND another Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card.</li>
<li><strong>5 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>User:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>At any point during another Player’s Turn, Attempt to take possession of the last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card. This Must be Attempted Once, and only Once, between Your PLAYABLE TURNs.</li>
<li>Failure to Attempt to do so or being prevented from doing so will result in all Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards being Collected and Re-Dealt according to Their Setup Rules.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li><strong>The Last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Holder:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>IF the User attempts to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Slap the Table to Defend Yourself.</li>
<li>IF You Slap the Table and the User did NOT attempt to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, You Forfeit it to them.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="special-victory-condition-note-box">
<strong>SPECICAL VICTORY CONDITION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>6 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> Possessing All 6 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards at once instantly Wins You the game [regardless of the number of Cards You possess; or whose Turn it actually is].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Any time a Player is Removed from or Restored to the Game by any means, Collect all Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, Shuffle and Re-Deal them to all Players still currently In the Game. But You Must maintain Play Order [as in the Player that caused this Removal/Restoration Effect (either directly or indirectly) Must perform the above Shuffle & Re-Deal of the Ancient Stones; and as the Dealer, they are last to receive one].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Special Victory Condition now Activates with by possessing all 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards in Play [Compare <a href="/01-a-crazy-8s-game-rulebook" class="matrix-link">(01-A) Soloverse 01-A Crazy 8's Game Rulebook: Cataclysm Expansion Additional Setup Rules</a>].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-the-architects-source-code">

<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Architect Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Persistent</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/19-the-architects-source-code.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a PLAYABLE TURN.</li>
<li>To use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Effect lasts for the rest of the game, or until it is Deactivated or Overwritten.
<ul>
<li>You May Deactivate this Card before you start any PLAYABLE TURN. To Deactivate this Card, Simply Remove it from the Game.</li>
<li>Should a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect be Activated, it will Overwrite this one, Removing it from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</li>

<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Change any 1 Rule in the Game, no Exceptions, for the Rest of the Game.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLES:</strong>
<ol>
<li>You Can change the Victory Condition to something like:
<ol type="a">
<li>All Players Must have 1 Card instead in Their Hand to Win.</li>
<li>All Players Must have 20 Cards instead in Their Hand to Win.</li>
<li>All Players Must Discard more than 7 Cards from Their Hand at any time to Win.</li>
<li>Prevent a Certain Player from Winning, regardless of existing Victory Conditions. They are not removed from the Game, They Must still Play and Draw a Card if They Cannot Play one.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>You could Prevent Certain Numbered Cards, a Certain Coloured Set of Cards or even Certain Classes of Cards from being Played.</li>
<li>What about forcing Players to Draw a Card before Starting Every Turn?</li>
<li>Now you have the Source Code of the Soloverse at Your Fingertips, You have Unlimited Access to change the Game's Rules as You see fit.</li>
<li>No Restrictions in What you Can Change in the Game's Rules, except for This Card's Rules, They Must all be Followed.</li>
</ol>
</div>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can only be Countered <strong>*** At the Moment of Activation ***</strong> by the Architect Chaos Card: <a href="#crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox" class="return-link">Unstable Soloversal Paradox</a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>UNIQUE RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>The Architect's Source Code Cannot change it's own Rules.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with a Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>

<li>To use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Effect lasts for the rest of the game, or until it is Deactivated or Overwritten.
<ul>
<li>You May Deactivate this Card before you start any PLAYABLE TURN. To Deactivate this Card, Simply Remove it from the Game.</li>
<li>Should a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect be Activated, it will Overwrite this one, Removing it from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="a">
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">For Yourself:</span></strong> Pure Chaos Cards become any Chaos Card in the Game You want when Played for the Rest of the Game.</li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Once per Game for each other Player:</span></strong> Any Chaos Card Played by any other Player is Played as a Pure Chaos Card for the that Turn.</li> 
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can only be Countered <strong>*** At the Moment of Activation ***</strong> by the Architect Chaos Card: <a href="#crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox" class="return-link">Unstable Soloversal Paradox</a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with this Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>

</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-the-soloverse-loophole">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">THE ARCHITECT OF THE SOLOVERSE: THE SOLOVERSE LOOPHOLE [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Architect of the Soloverse Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Architect Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/19-the-soloverse-loophole.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a PLAYABLE TURN.</li>
<li>This Card Can be Used Two Times in Any Game.</li>
<li>The First Time You Use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Second Time You Use this Card, resolve it's Effects then Remove this Card from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Declare a Number [0-9].</li>
<li>Discard the Top card of the Deck until a Number Card [0-9] is Revealed.</li>
<li>The Number that is Revealed determines one of the following Effects:
<ol type="a">
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Any Other Number:</span></strong> The Number of Cards all other Players Draw.</li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Number You Declared:</span></strong> You Win Instantly.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can be Countered <strong>*** At the Moment of Activation or the Moment of it's Special Victory Condition Activating***</strong> by the Architect Chaos Card: <a href="#crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox" class="return-link">Unstable Soloversal Paradox</a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with a Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-unrestriction-stone">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">UNRESTRICTION STONE [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Cataclysm Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Ancient Stone Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Restriction Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/16-cataclysm-expansion-6-of-6-unrestriction-stone.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>To use an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Turn it Sideways.</li>
<li>Each Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card’s Effect Can only be used Once Each Turn; however, You Can use it on additional Turns afterwards.</li>
<li>Use of Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects do NOT count towards Your one Chaos Card per Turn Restriction.</li>
<li>Activate before You start a PLAYABLE TURN.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ul>
<li>When activated, the User Can choose to disable the Restrictions of all Their Chaos Cards [and I mean all Restrictions of all Chaos Cards; so, remember to consider the “Class Restrictions” NOT just the individual “Card Restrictions”].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COMMUTATION OF THE ANCIENTS EFFECT:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If at the end of Your Turn You chose to use the above ability, this Effect activates:
<ol>
<li>First, collect all Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards and return them to the Cataclysm Side Deck and Shuffle it.</li>
<li>Next, Remove 1 Player of Your Choice from the Game.</li>
<li>Then, if more than one Player has previously been Removed from the Game by any means, You Must choose one to Restore to the Game.</li>
<li>Re-Deal all Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards to all Players now currently in the Game, but You Must maintain Play Order [See: RESTRICTION below].</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>UNIQUE RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>The Unrestriction Stone Cannot disable its own Restrictions.</li>
<li>You Cannot receive any more Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards again for the rest of the game.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot be Countered, Including Their Special Victory Condition.</li>
<li>Only 1 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Can be used each Turn [compare BULK POSSESSION NOTES].</li>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot affect the same Players two Activations in a row, regardless of who is the User and who is the Target.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If someone uses the Teleportation Stone to take control of Your seat and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, the Teleportation Stone Cannot reclaim them. You Must use the Teleportation Stone to Target another Player.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Individual Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards Can only Target the other Player every second Turn.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards May NOT count towards Your Hand’s Card count [so You Can still win with no Cards even if You possess one], but they Can still be stolen via card Swapping means; however, if doing this, You Can only Swap Hand Cards or Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If chosen, Paradigm Shift’s Group Targeting Swap Effect Can target all Player’s Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards instead of Their Hand Cards [the Swap includes All Players Except Previous Unrestriction Stone Users, regardless of whether they currently own an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card or not].</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>Furthermore, Hand Discarding Effects affect both Hand and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. However, Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards are collected, Shuffled and Re-Dealt according to Their Setup Rules.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Card Effects like Black Hole.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>BULK POSSESSION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>2 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 3 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES NOT include the ability to play Another Chaos Card or Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>3 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card but DOES NOT include the ability to play another Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>4 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 5 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card AND another Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card.</li>
<li><strong>5 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>User:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>At any point during another Player’s Turn, Attempt to take possession of the last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card. This Must be Attempted Once, and only Once, between Your PLAYABLE TURNs.</li>
<li>Failure to Attempt to do so or being prevented from doing so will result in all Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards being Collected and Re-Dealt according to Their Setup Rules.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li><strong>The Last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Holder:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>IF the User attempts to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Slap the Table to Defend Yourself.</li>
<li>IF You Slap the Table and the User did NOT attempt to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, You Forfeit it to them.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="special-victory-condition-note-box">
<strong>SPECICAL VICTORY CONDITION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>6 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> Possessing All 6 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards at once instantly Wins You the game [regardless of the number of Cards You possess; or whose Turn it actually is].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Any time a Player is Removed from or Restored to the Game by any means, Collect all Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, Shuffle and Re-Deal them to all Players still currently In the Game. But You Must maintain Play Order [as in the Player that caused this Removal/Restoration Effect (either directly or indirectly) Must perform the above Shuffle & Re-Deal of the Ancient Stones; and as the Dealer, they are last to receive one].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Special Victory Condition now Activates with by possessing all 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards in Play [Compare <a href="/01-a-crazy-8s-game-rulebook" class="matrix-link">(01-A) Soloverse 01-A Crazy 8's Game Rulebook: Cataclysm Expansion Additional Setup Rules</a>].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">THE ARCHITECT OF THE SOLOVERSE: UNSTABLE SOLOVERSAL PARADOX [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Architect of the Soloverse Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Architect Class & Counter Class LV:5</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">

</div>
</div>

<ul>
<li>This Card Can be Used Two Times in Any Game.</li>
<li>The First Time You Use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Second Time You Use this Card, resolve it's Effects then Remove this Card from the Game.</li>

<li>Counter Any Counter Class LV:1-4 Chaos Card Effect.</li>
<li>Counter Any Architect Class Chaos Card Effect.</li>
<li>Counter Any Victory Condition, force the Winning Player to Continue the Game by Drawing 5 New Cards. In the Case of Special Victory Conditions [Example: Unification Class, Ancient Stone Class, etc...], remove said Chaos Cards from the Game.</li>
<li>Counter Being Removed from the Game by any means.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Card Can Counter ALL OTHER CHAOS CARD EFFECTS, regardless of if those Chaos Cards Could NOT be Countered otherwise.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>UNIQUE RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be Countered by any means.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with a Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-virus">

<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">VIRUS [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Pandemic Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Trap Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Stackable</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">User Goes Last</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/11-pandemic-expansion-trap-01-virus-all.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>When Revealed, remove from the Deck Temporarily.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>In Turn Order all Players Draw 1 Card.</li>
<li>Continue to Draw if required.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Shuffle Virus back into the Deck.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Can only be Countered by <a href="#crazy-8s-cure" class="return-link">Cure's</a> Effect..</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-white-hole">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">WHITE HOLE [x2]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Extension</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Normal Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">User Goes Last</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/01-core-extension-white-hole.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Play to the side of the Discards to use this Card's Effect [not if using it as a Pure Chaos Card (any Type) ].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Choose one:
<ol type="a">
<li>White Hole's Effect:
<ol type="i">
<li>All Players in Play Order secretly choose any Card from the Discards and adds it to Their Hand.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Or simply Play as a <a href="#crazy-8s-pure-chaos" class="return-link">Pure Chaos Card</a> [any Type].</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Then place this Card on top of the Discards.</li>
<li>Choose a Colour to Continue.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<>
<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />

<!-- Permanent Cache-Busting Network Headers for Clean Vercel Deployments -->
<meta http-equiv="Cache-Control" content="no-cache, no-store, Must-revalidate" />
<meta http-equiv="Pragma" content="no-cache" />
<meta http-equiv="Expires" content="0" />

<!-- ==================== SOLOVERSE VAULT CENTRAL STYLING ENGINE ==================== -->
<style is:inline>
.hero-center {
display: flex;
flex-direction: column;
align-items: center; /* Centers items horizontally */
text-align: center; /* Centers inline multi-line text */
gap: 10px; /* Adds clean spacing between h1 and p */
margin: 40px auto; /* Generates balance margin space around block */
}

:root, [data-theme="dark"] {
--bg-main: #0f1015;
--bg-card: #16171e;
--bg-main-box: #1c1e27;
--text-primary: #e4e6eb;
--text-muted: #a4a6b5;
--text-body: #ced4da;
--text-divider: #2a2d3d;
--color-link: #3b82f6;
}

body { 
background-color: var(--bg-main); 
color: var(--text-primary); 
font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif; 
margin: 0; 
padding: 20px; 
max-width: 100vw;
overflow-x: hidden;
}

.vault-container { 
max-width: 1400px; 
margin: 0 auto; 
width: 100%;
}

header { 
text-align: center; 
margin-bottom: 40px; 
border-bottom: 1px solid #2a2d3d; 
padding-bottom: 25px; 
}

/* 1B. THEME PILL TOGGLE INTERFACE CONTROLS */
.theme-toggle-container {
display: flex;
justify-content: flex-start;
align-items: center;
gap: 10px;
margin-bottom: 25px;
}

.theme-btn {
background: #171922;
color: #ffffff;
border: 1px solid #2d3139;
padding: 10px 16px;
border-radius: 50px; 
font-weight: 700;
cursor: pointer;
font-size: 0.9rem;
display: inline-flex; 
align-items: center;
gap: 8px;
transition: background 0.2s, border-color 0.2s, transform 0.1s;
}

.theme-btn:hover {
border-color: #3b82f6;
background: #1c1d26;
}

.theme-btn:active { 
transform: scale(0.97); 
}

/* 2. DYNAMIC SELECTION CARDS GRID PROFILE */
.grid { 
display: grid; 
grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr)); 
gap: 20px; 
width: 100%; 
margin-bottom: 40px;
}

.game-card {
background: #16171e;
border-radius: 16px; 
overflow: hidden;
border: 1px solid #2d3139; 
box-shadow: 0 10px 25px -5px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
transition: transform 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1), box-shadow 0.2s ease;
display: flex;
flex-direction: column;
}
.game-card:hover {
transform: translateY(-4px);
box-shadow: 0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
}

.game-header {
padding: 24px 20px;
text-align: center;
position: relative; 
/* 🚀 THE ZOOM FIX: Pre-rounds the inner color block so fractional scaling remainders can't bleed out */
border-top-left-radius: 14px;
border-top-right-radius: 14px;
background-clip: padding-box; /* Forces the background color to respect inner boundary layers */
}
.game-header::after {
content: '';
position: absolute;
inset: 0;
background: linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12), rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35));
pointer-events: none; 
}

.game-title {
position: relative;
z-index: 10; 
margin: 0;
font-size: 1.15rem;
font-weight: 800;
text-transform: uppercase;
letter-spacing: 0.03em;
}

.box-contents {
padding: 20px;
background: #1c1e27; 
flex-grow: 1;
}

/* 3. SPLIT-VIEW OPERATIONS INTERFACE DASHBOARD */
.vault-panels-holder { width: 100%; }
.game-panel { 
animation: vaultFadeIn 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1); 
width: 100%;
}
.hidden { display: none !important; }

/* ==========================================================================
 3. SPLIT-VIEW OPERATIONS INTERFACE DASHBOARD (RE-CENTERED & VIEWPORT-LOCKED)
 ========================================================================= */
.vault-panels-holder { 
width: 100%; 
max-width: 1400px;
margin: 40px auto 0 auto; /* Perfectly re-centers the core panel layout tray */
box-sizing: border-box;
}

.game-panel { 
animation: vaultFadeIn 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1); 
width: 100%;
}

.hidden { 
display: none !important; 
}

/* 📱 DEFAULT PORTRAIT VIEWPORT SETTING */
.split-view.flex-layout-engine { 
display: flex;
flex-direction: column;
gap: 20px; 
margin-top: 20px; 
width: 100%;
height: 75vh; /* Sets a locked canvas container for scrolling columns */
}

/* 🕹️ LOCKED MULTI-SCROLL INDEPENDENT COLUMNS (ONLY active for multi-doc layouts) */
.split-view.flex-layout-engine .content-block { 
background: #16171e; 
border: 1px solid #2a2d3d; 
padding: 20px; 
border-radius: 8px; 
min-width: 0; 
display: none; /* Controlled dynamically by data-active-layout attributes */
flex-direction: column;
flex: 1;
overflow-y: auto; 
-webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch; 
box-shadow: inset 0 0 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
}

/* 🎮 AUTOMATIC LANDSCAPE BREAKPOINT FOR SIDE-BY-SIDE SIDE PANEL GRID ENGINE */
@media screen and (min-width: 600px) and (orientation: landscape) { 
.split-view.flex-layout-engine:not(.single-column) { 
display: grid !important;
grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; /* Splits the view perfectly 50/50 */
gap: 25px;
height: 80vh; 
}
.split-view.flex-layout-engine:not(.single-column) .content-block {
height: 100%; 
}
}

/* ⚡ INTERACTIVE RENDERING STATE STATES ENGINE CONTROLLER */
[data-active-layout="rules-cards"] .view-slot-rules,
[data-active-layout="rules-cards"] .view-slot-cards { display: flex !important; }

[data-active-layout="solo-cards"] .view-slot-solo,
[data-active-layout="solo-cards"] .view-slot-cards { display: flex !important; }

[data-active-layout="rules-solo"] .view-slot-rules,
[data-active-layout="rules-solo"] .view-slot-solo { display: flex !important; }

/* 📖 AUTOMATIC SINGLE-COLUMN DROPOUT MECHANICS */
/* If a game only has 1 rulebook, it breaks out of the locked viewport box and scrolls naturally */
.split-view.flex-layout-engine.single-column {
display: flex !important;
flex-direction: column !important;
height: auto !important; 
}
.split-view.flex-layout-engine.single-column .content-block {
display: flex !important;
overflow-y: visible !important; 
height: auto !important;
box-shadow: none !important;
}

/* Explicit single-view rules when manually toggled from options dropdown */
[data-active-layout="rules-only"] .view-slot-rules { display: flex !important; flex: 1 1 100% !important; }
[data-active-layout="cards-only"] .view-slot-cards { display: flex !important; flex: 1 1 100% !important; }
[data-active-layout="solo-only"] .view-slot-solo { display: flex !important; flex: 1 1 100% !important; }

[data-active-layout="rules-only"] .split-view.flex-layout-engine,
[data-active-layout="cards-only"] .split-view.flex-layout-engine,
[data-active-layout="solo-only"] .split-view.flex-layout-engine {
display: flex !important;
flex-direction: column !important;
height: auto !important;
}
[data-active-layout="rules-only"] .content-block,
[data-active-layout="cards-only"] .content-block,
[data-active-layout="solo-only"] .content-block {
overflow-y: visible !important;
height: auto !important;
box-shadow: none !important;
}

/* ==========================================================================
 8. UNIFIED MEDIA ENGINE & VISUAL CARD GRIDS (PARENT TARGETING FIX)
 ========================================================================= */
.text-layer .image-container,
.game-panel .image-container {
margin: 20px auto !important;
padding: 12px !important;
background: #1c1d26 !important; 
border: 1px solid #2a2d3d !important;
border-radius: 8px !important;
text-align: center !important;
max-width: 100% !important;
display: block !important;
flex-shrink: 0 !important;
box-shadow: 0 4px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.5) !important;
}

.text-layer .image-container img,
.game-panel .image-container img {
max-width: 100% !important;
max-height: 450px !important;
width: auto !important;
height: auto !important;
object-fit: contain !important;
border-radius: 6px !important;
margin: 0 auto !important;
display: block !important;
}

.text-layer .card-top-section .image-container img,
.game-panel .card-top-section .image-container img {
max-width: 150px !important;
height: auto !important;
}

.image-caption {
font-size: 0.85rem;
color: #a4a6b5;
margin-top: 10px;
font-weight: 500;
line-height: 1.4;
}

.image-row-container {
display: flex !important;
flex-wrap: wrap !important;
gap: 15px !important;
justify-content: center !important;
margin: 20px 0 !important;
width: 100% !important;
}
.image-row-container .image-container {
flex: 1 1 calc(33.333% - 15px) !important;
min-width: 220px !important;
margin: 0 !important;
}

/* COLOR COATINGS HOOKS */
.text-layer .image-container.border-steel, .game-panel .image-container.border-steel { border: 3px solid #1d4ed8 !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-poison-core, .game-panel .image-container.border-poison-core { border: 3px solid #6b21a8 !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-venom-core, .game-panel .image-container.border-venom-core { border: 3px solid #dc2626 !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-carbon, .game-panel .image-container.border-carbon { border: 3px solid #ea580c !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-stinger-core, .game-panel .image-container.border-stinger-core { border: 3px solid #eab308 !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-toxin-core, .game-panel .image-container.border-toxin-core { border: 3px solid #16a34a !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-diamond, .game-panel .image-container.border-diamond { border: 3px solid #3b82f6 !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-emerald, .game-panel .image-container.border-emerald { border: 3px solid #10b981; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-redwood, .game-panel .image-container.border-redwood { border: 3px solid #5c4033 !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-oak, .game-panel .image-container.border-oak { border: 3px solid #b58900 !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-rose, .game-panel .image-container.border-rose { border: 3px solid #ec4899 !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-amethyst, .game-panel .image-container.border-amethyst { border: 3px solid #8b5cf6 !important; }
.text-layer .image-container.border-stone, .game-panel .image-container.border-stone { border: 3px solid #6b7280 !important; }
		
.text-layer .card-top-section,
.game-panel .card-top-section {
display: flex !important;
justify-content: space-between !important;
align-items: flex-start !important;
gap: 20px !important;
width: 100% !important;
}

.text-layer .card-header-block,
.game-panel .card-header-block {
flex: 1 !important;
}

/* ==========================================================================
 7. UNIFIED NAVIGATION LINKS & JUMP MATRIX INTERACTION
 ========================================================================= */
/* Meta tag positioning layout */
.meta-tag {
font-size: 0.9rem;
color: var(--text-muted);
margin-bottom: 15px;
font-weight: 600;
}

/* Global Navigation Control Elements */
.platform-hub-nav {
margin-bottom: 25px;
}

.hub-link-button,
.hub-link-button:visited,
.hub-link-button:link {
display: inline-flex;
align-items: center;
background: var(--bg-main-box);
border: 1px solid var(--text-divider);
color: var(--color-link) !important;
padding: 10px 18px;
border-radius: 8px;
text-decoration: none;
font-weight: 700;
font-size: 0.9rem;
transition: all 0.2s ease-in-out;
}

.hub-link-button:hover {
background: var(--text-divider);
color: var(--text-primary) !important;
transform: translateY(-1px);
}

/* Index Jump Matrix Table Container */
.matrix-toc-box {
background: var(--bg-card);
border: 1px solid var(--text-divider);
padding: 24px;
border-radius: 12px;
margin: 30px 0;
}

.matrix-toc-title {
margin: 0 0 15px 0;
color: var(--color-link);
font-size: 1.15rem;
font-weight: 700;
text-transform: uppercase;
letter-spacing: 0.02em;
}

.matrix-toc-grid {
list-style: none;
padding: 0;
margin: 0;
display: flex;
flex-direction: column;
gap: 12px;
}

/* Sub-grid variant used by the contents matrix on this page only */
.matrix-toc-sub-grid {
list-style: none;
padding: 0;
margin: 0;
display: grid;
grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(160px, 1fr));
gap: 12px;
align-items: start;
}

/* Matrix items styled as compact cards for denser layout */
.matrix-toc-sub-grid > li {
background: var(--bg-main-box);
border: 1px solid var(--text-divider);
border-radius: 8px;
box-shadow: none;
display: flex; 
overflow: hidden; 
}

/* Scoped link styles so other uses of .matrix-link aren't affected */
.matrix-toc-sub-grid .matrix-link {
display: flex;
align-items: center;
justify-content: center;
width: 100%; 
min-height: 100%; 
padding: 10px 12px; 
color: var(--text-primary);
text-decoration: none;
font-weight: 700;
text-align: center;
box-sizing: border-box; 
transition: all 0.12s ease-in-out;
}

.matrix-toc-sub-grid .matrix-link:hover {
color: var(--color-link);
text-decoration: underline;
}

.matrix-link {
color: var(--text-muted);
text-decoration: none;
font-weight: 600;
transition: color 0.15s;
}

.matrix-link:hover {
color: var(--color-link);
text-decoration: underline;
}

/* Bottom Step Jump Elements */
.return-link-wrapper {
text-align: right;
margin-top: 15px;
margin-bottom: 35px;
}

.return-link,
.return-link:visited,
.return-link:link {
display: inline-flex;
align-items: center;
background: var(--bg-main-box);
border: 1px solid var(--text-divider);
color: var(--color-link) !important; 
padding: 8px 14px; 
border-radius: 8px;
text-decoration: none;
font-size: 0.85rem;
font-weight: 700;
transition: all 0.2s ease-in-out;
}

.return-link:hover {
background: var(--text-divider);
color: var(--text-primary) !important;
transform: translateY(-1px);
text-decoration: none;
}

/* DROPDOWN CUSTOM ACCENT ADJUSTMENTS */
.layout-dropdown.rules-box {
position: absolute;
right: 0;
top: calc(100% + 8px);
z-index: 500;
width: 260px;
box-shadow: 0 15px 35px rgba(0,0,0,0.7);
display: flex;
flex-direction: column;
}

.layout-opt-btn.rules-box {
color: #e4e6eb;
text-align: left;
cursor: pointer;
font-size: 0.85rem;
font-weight: 600;
width: 100%;
box-sizing: border-box;
}

.layout-opt-btn.rules-box:hover {
background: #2a2d3d !important;
border-color: #3b82f6 !important;
color: #3b82f6 !important;
}

/* 📋 SPECIALIZED DROPDOWN CONTAINER MATRIX */
.rules-popup-box {
position: absolute;
right: 0; /* Flush alignment on desktop */
top: calc(100% + 8px);
z-index: 500;
width: 260px;
background: #1c1d26;
padding: 12px;
border-radius: 6px;
border: 1px solid #2a2d3d;
box-shadow: 0 15px 35px rgba(0,0,0,0.7);
display: flex;
flex-direction: column;
box-sizing: border-box;
}

/* 📱 RESPONSIVE MOBILE OVERFLOW INJECTION SAFEGUARD */
@media screen and (max-width: 480px) {
.rules-popup-box {
right: -10px; /* Snaps closer to the viewport boundary lines */
width: 82vw; /* Dynamically restricts width based on mobile display scales */
max-width: 250px;
box-shadow: 0 10px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.8);
}
}

.layout-opt-btn.popup-item {
color: #e4e6eb;
text-align: left;
cursor: pointer;
font-size: 0.85rem;
font-weight: 600;
width: 100%;
margin: 0;
padding: 10px;
background: #20222e;
border: 1px solid #2a2d3d;
border-radius: 6px;
box-sizing: border-box;
transition: all 0.15s ease;
}

.layout-opt-btn.popup-item:hover {
background: #2a2d3d !important;
border-color: #3b82f6 !important;
color: #3b82f6 !important;
}

/* 🚀 THE MOBILE HEADER BUTTON FLUSH-RIGHT RE-ALIGNMENT ENGINE */
.panel-accent-header {
display: flex !important; 
justify-content: space-between !important; 
align-items: center !important; 
flex-wrap: wrap !important; 
gap: 15px !important;
}

/* Forces the view menu layout button container to float right when items wrap on mobile viewports */
.panel-accent-header .layout-menu-container {
margin-left: auto !important; 
}

/* Cleaned up system popover boundary tracker */
.rules-popup-box {
position: absolute;
right: 0 !important; /* Snaps flush right with your button border perfectly */
top: calc(100% + 8px);
z-index: 500;
width: 260px;
background: #1c1d26;
padding: 12px;
border-radius: 6px;
border: 1px solid #2a2d3d;
box-shadow: 0 15px 35px rgba(0,0,0,0.7);
display: flex;
flex-direction: column;
box-sizing: border-box;
}

/* ==========================================================================
 12. UNIFIED RULEBOOK MARKDOWN COMPONENT ENGINE (.text-layer)
 ========================================================================= */

/* 🎨 VERIFIED BACKUP PALETTE ENGINE INITIALISATION */
.text-layer {
/* Core Document Typography Colors */
--color-card-title: #0000ff; 
--color-card-class: #8cfffb; 
--accent-heading: #3a3f54;
--accent-subheading: #3b82f6;

/* 🟢 1. TIPS CONTAINER (Original Light Green) */
--border-tip: #aded8f; 
--bg-tip: #aded8f30;

/* 🟢 2. GENERAL NOTES CONTAINER (Original Green) */
--border-note: #0ed145;
--bg-note: #0ed14530;

/* 🟡 3. METAGAME RULES EXAMPLES CONTAINER (Original Yellow) */
--border-example: #fff200; 
--bg-example: #fff20030;

/* 🟠 4. DISCARDS POSITIONING ACTIONS CONTAINER (Original Orange) */
--border-discards: #ff7f27;
--bg-discards: #ff7f2730;

/* 🟤 5. CHALLENGE SYSTEM NOTES CONTAINER (Original Light Brown) */
--border-challenge: #b97a56; 
--bg-challenge: #b97a5630;

/* 🟤 6. CRITICAL METAGAME INFORMATION CONTAINER (Original Dark Brown) */
--border-important: #84471a; 
--bg-important: #84471a30;

/* 🔴 7. RESTRICTIONS & ILLEGAL PLAY PROTECTIONS CONTAINER (Original Red) */
--border-restriction: #ec1c24; 
--bg-restriction: #ec1c2430;

/* 💗 8. COMPATIBILITY & SEQUENCE NOTES CONTAINER (Original Pink) */
--border-compatibility: #ffaec8; 
--bg-compatibility: #ffaec830;

/* 🍇 9. SPECIAL VICTORY ENGINE CONDITIONS CONTAINER (Original Light Purple) */
--border-victory: #b497ef; 
--bg-victory: #b497ef30;

/* 🔮 10. TWO-PLAYER BALANCE VARIATION MODULES CONTAINER (Original Purple) */
--border-two-player: #9d00ff;
--bg-two-player: #9d00ff30;

/* 🪙 11. ADVANCED OPTIONAL HOUSE MODIFIERS CONTAINER (Original Grey) */
--border-advanced: #b2b2b2;
--bg-advanced: #b2b2b230;
}

/* 📖 TYPOGRAPHY & STRUCTURAL HEADINGS RESETS */
.text-layer h2.rulebook-heading {
font-size: 1.5rem;
color: var(--text-primary);
margin-top: 35px;
margin-bottom: 12px;
border-bottom: 2px solid var(--accent-heading);
padding-bottom: 8px;
font-weight: 700;
text-transform: uppercase;
}

.text-layer h3.rulebook-subheading {
font-size: 1.2rem;
color: var(--text-primary);
margin-top: 25px;
margin-bottom: 12px;
border-left: 4px solid var(--accent-subheading);
padding-left: 12px;
